Quality is a free text label written with either case\n   380→# and with a few typos, so it is matched in lower case.\n   381→\n   382→def _quality(cluster):\n   383→    \"\"\"Quality label of a cluster; a handful carry no label at all.\"\"\"\n   384→    label = cluster['quality']\n   385→    return label.strip().lower() if isinstance(label, str) else ''\n   386→\n   387→\n   388→class Neural:\n   389→    \"\"\"The QC-passing units of a session, as smoothed firing rates.\"\"\"\n   390→\n   391→    def __init__(self, obj, probes, trials):\n   392→        self.obj = obj\n   393→        self.go_cue = trial_column(obj['bp'], 'ev', 'goCue')\n   394→        self.clusters = [cluster for probe in probes                # probes concatenated\n   395→                         for cluster in self._probe_clusters(probe)\n   396→                         if _quality(cluster) not in QUALITY_DROP]\n   397→\n   398→        # two sessions keep running after the recording stops, leaving trials with no spikes\n   399→        last = max(int(np.asarray(cluster['trial'], int).max(initial=0))\n   400→                   for cluster in self.clusters)\n   401→        self.trials = np.asarray([t for t in trials if t < last])   # trial numbers are 1 based\n   402→\n   403→    def _probe_clusters(self, probe):\n   404→        \"\"\"One dict per cluster on the given probe, however the file stores them.\"\"\"\n   405→        entry = self.obj['clu'][probe - 1]\n   406→        if isinstance(entry, dict) and isinstance(entry['tm'], list):\n   407→            # v7.3: one entry per probe, each field holding one array per cluster\n   408→            return [{field: entry[field][i] for field in entry}\n   409→                    for i in range(len(entry['tm']))]\n   410→        return self.obj['clu']                     # v5: one entry per cluster, single probe\n   411→\n   412→    def spike_count(self, cluster):\n   413→        \"\"\"Spikes of one cluster per trial and bin, aligned to the go cue.\"\"\"\n   414→        spike_trial = np.asarray(cluster['trial'], int) - 1         # trial numbers are 1 based\n   415→        spike_time = np.asarray(cluster['trialtm'], float) - self.go_cue[spike_trial]\n   416→\n   417→        # count spikes into a trial by bin grid; those outside the window fall off the edges\n   418→        trial_edges = np.arange(self.go_cue.size + 1) - 0.5\n   419→        counts, _, _ = np.histogram2d(spike_trial, spike_time, bins=[trial_edges, BIN_EDGES])\n   420→        return counts[self.trials]                                  # only the trials we keep\n   421→\n   422→    def rates(self):\n   423→        \"\"\"Firing rate of every unit above MIN_RATE, as (n_units, n_trials, N_BINS).\"\"\"\n   424→        sigma = RATE_SD_MS / 1000 / BIN\n   425→        rates = []\n   426→        for cluster in self.clusters:\n   427→            rate = self.spike_count(cluster) / BIN                 # (n_trials, 1, N_BINS)\n   457→\n   458→    return {\n   459→        'session_id': name,\n   460→        'subject': name.split('_')[0],\n   461→        'neural': [np.ascontiguousarray(rates[:, t, :]) for t in range(n_trials)],\n   462→        'input': [inp[t] for t in range(n_trials)],\n   463→        'output': [out[t] for t in range(n_trials)],\n   464→        'n_trials': n_trials,\n   465→        'n_units': int(rates.shape[0]),\n   466→    }\n   467→\n   468→\n   469→def assemble(results):\n   470→    \"\"\"Stitch per-session results into the target dictionary.\"\"\"\n   471→    subjects = sorted({r['subject'] for r in results})\n   472→    sub_ix = {s: i for i, s in enumerate(subjects)}\n   473→\n   474→    return {\n   475→        'neural': [r['neural'] for r in results],\n   476→        'input': [r['input'] for r in results],\n   477→        'output': [r['output'] for r in results],\n   478→\n   479→        'subjects': subjects,\n   480→        'subject_idx': np.array([sub_ix[r['subject']] for r in results], np.int32),\n   481→\n   482→        'brain_regions': [BRAIN_REGION],\n   483→        'brain_region_idx': [np.zeros(r['n_units'], np.int32) for r in results],\n   484→\n   485→        'input_names': INPUT_NAMES,\n   486→        'output_names': OUTPUT_NAMES,\n   487→        'output_values': OUTPUT_VALUES,\n   488→\n   489→        'metadata': {\n   490→            'task_description':\n   491→                'Head-fixed mice lick left or right for water. struct\n        return {k: _h5(f, node[k]) for k in node}\n    if node.attrs.get('MATLAB_class', b'').decode() == 'char':\n        return ''.join(chr(c) for c in np.asarray(node[()]).ravel())\n    if node.dtype == object:                                # Early-lick and photostim trials\n# are dropped, following the paper. The index keeps the original trial numbers, which\n# every later step uses to reach back into the raw arrays.\n\ndef trial_column(bp, *names):\n    \"\"\"One entry per trial from a field of bp, e.g. trial_column(bp, 'stim', 'enable').\"\"\"\n    n_trials = int(np.ravel(bp['Ntrials'])[0])\n    field = bp\n    for name in names:\n        field = field[name]\n    return np.ravel(np.asarray(field, float))[:n_trials]\n\n\ndef trial_info(bp):\n    \"\"\"Outcome, context, and lick direction per trial, without early-lick or photostim trials.\"\"\"\n    hit = trial_column(bp, 'hit') > 0\n    miss = trial_column(bp, 'miss') > 0\n    right = trial_column(bp, 'R') > 0\n    autowater = trial_column(bp, 'autowater') > 0\n    early = trial_column(bp, 'early') > 0\n    stim = trial_column(bp, 'stim', 'enable') > 0\n    n_trials = hit.size\n\n    # a hit is a correct lick and a miss an incorrect one; the animal ignored the rest\n    outcome = np.full(n_trials, OUTCOME['ignore'])\n    outcome[hit] = OUTCOME['correct']\n    outcome[miss] = OUTCOME['incorrect']\n\n    # water is given from a random port without any cue in the WC context\n    context = np.where(autowater, CONTEXT['WC'], CONTEXT['DR'])\n\n    # a hit licks the instructed port, a miss licks the other one, an ignore neither\n    lick_direction = np.full(n_trials, LICK['no lick'])\n    lick_direction[hit] = np.where(right[hit], LICK['right'], LICK['left'])\n    lick_direction[miss] = np.where(right[miss], LICK['left'], LICK['right'])\n\n    info = pd.DataFrame({'lick_direction': lick_direction, 'context': context,\n                         'outcome': outcome})\n    info.index.name = 'trial'\n    return info[~early & ~stim]\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------- camera --\n# Tongue velocity, paw velocity, and motion energy for one session. A class because the\n# video offset, the per-view scales, and the median split are session-wide\n# quantities that every trial reuses.\n\ndef _feature(traj, index):\n    \"\"\"x, y, and likelihood of one feature, whichever way the file orders ts.\"\"\"\n    ts = np.asarray(traj['ts'])\n    if ts.shape[0] == len(traj['featNames']):      # v7.3 reads it as (features, xyl, frames)\n        return ts[index]\n    return ts[:, :, index].T                       # v5 keeps MATLAB's (frames, xyl, features)\n\n\ndef _runs(valid):\n    \"\"\"First and last+1 index of each contiguous run of valid frames.\"\"\"\n    edges = np.diff(np.concatenate([[0], valid.astype(int), [0]]))\n    return zip(np.flatnonzero(edges == 1), np.flatnonzero(edges == -1))\n\n\ndef _bin_frames(time, values):\n    \"\"\"Mean of the valid frames in each bin, NaN where a bin holds no valid frame.\"\"\"\n    bin_of_frame = np.searchsorted(BIN_EDGES, time, side='right') - 1\n    valid = np.isfinite(values) & (bin_of_frame >= 0) & (bin_of_frame < N_BINS)\n    total = np.bincount(bin_of_frame[valid], weights=values[valid], minlength=N_BINS)\n    count = np.bincount(bin_of_frame[valid], minlength=N_BINS)\n    return np.where(count > 0, total / np.maximum(count, 1), np.nan)\n\n\ndef _mean_over_available(first, second):\n    \"\"\"Mean of the two, whichever one is there alone, or NaN where neither is.\"\"\"\n    stacked = np.stack([first, second])\n    count = np.sum(np.isfinite(stacked), axis=0)\n    total = np.nansum(stacked, axis=0)\n    return np.where(count > 0, total / np.maximum(count, 1), np.nan)\n\n\ndef _discretize(values, threshold):\n    \"\"\"Two classes split at the threshold; untracked bins take the trailing class.\"\"\"\n    code = np.where(values >= threshold, MOVEMENT['above threshold'], MOVEMENT['below threshold'])\n    return np.where(np.isnan(values), MOVEMENT['not visible'], code)\n\n\nclass Camera:\n    \"\"\"The three camera streams of a session, binned and cut into classes.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, obj, name, trials, datadir):\n        self.obj = obj\n        self.trials = list(trials)\n        self.go_cue = trial_column(obj['bp'], 'ev', 'goCue')\n        self.offset = self._video_offset()             # one constant for the whole session\n        self.energy = load_motion_energy(name, datadir)\n\n        # frame resolution velocity, for the two tongue views and for the paw\n        self.side_tongue = self._trial_velocity(SIDE_TONGUE)\n        self.bottom_tongue = self._trial_velocity(BOTTOM_TONGUE)\n        self.paw = self._trial_velocity(PAW)\n\n        # the two cameras see the tongue at different pixel scales\n        self.side_scale = self._view_scale(self.side_tongue)\n        self.bottom_scale = self._view_scale(self.bottom_tongue)\n\n    def _trial_velocity(self, feature):\n        \"\"\"Frame resolution velocity of one tracked feature, one entry per trial.\"\"\"\n        return {trial: self._velocity(self._track(trial, feature)) for trial in self.trials}\n\n    def _video_offset(self):\n        \"\"\"Seconds by which the video clock leads the behavior clock (findVideoOffset.m).\"\"\"\n        sample_rate = float(np.ravel(self.obj['sglx']['fs'])[0])\n        video_start = np.ravel(self.obj['sglx']['bitcode']['bitstart']) / sample_rate\n        behavior_start = trial_column(self.obj['bp'], 'ev', 'bitStart')\n        return pd.Series(video_start).mode().iloc[0] - pd.Series(behavior_start).mode().iloc[0]\n\n    def _traj(self, trial, view):\n        \"\"\"Tracking of one trial and camera, however the file stores it.\"\"\"\n        traj = self.obj['traj'][view]\n        if isinstance(traj, dict):                     # v7.3: one entry per camera\n            return {field: traj[field][trial] for field in traj}\n        entry = traj[trial]                            # v5: one entry per trial\n        if hasattr(entry, '_fieldnames'):              # scipy leaves nested structs as objects\n            return {field: getattr(entry, field) for field in entry._fieldnames}\n        return entry\n\n    # camera frame time after correcting for the video offset and aligning to the go cue, in seconds\n    def _frame_time(self, trial, view):\n        \"\"\"Frame times of one trial and camera, in seconds from go cue onset.\"\"\"\n        frame_times = np.ravel(self._traj(trial, view)['frameTimes'])\n        return frame_times - self.offset - self.go_cue[trial]\n\n    def _camera_of(self, trial, feature):\n        \"\"\"The camera that tracks a feature, and its tracking for this trial.\"\"\"\n        for view in (SIDE, BOTTOM):\n            traj = self._traj(trial, view)\n            if feature in list(traj['featNames']):     # the name picks out the camera\n                return view, traj\n        raise KeyError('%s is tracked by neither camera' % feature)\n\n    # x, y, and likelihood within the time window\n    def _track(self, trial, feature):\n        \"\"\"x, y, and likelihood of one feature over the frames inside the window.\"\"\"\n        view, traj = self._camera_of(trial, feature)\n        x, y, likelihood = _feature(traj, list(traj['featNames']).index(feature))\n        time = self._frame_time(trial, view)           # frame counts can differ between views\n        inside = (time >= T_START) & (time <= T_STOP)\n        return time[inside], x[inside], y[inside], likelihood[inside]\n\n    # smooth the x, y postion, and compute the speed, filter by the likelihood threshold > 0.90\n    def _velocity(self, track):\n        \"\"\"Combined velocity over the valid frames, NaN elsewhere.\"\"\"\n        time, x, y, likelihood = track\n        speed = np.full(time.size, np.nan)\n        if time.size < 2:                              # a few trials have no frame times\n            return time, speed\n\n        sigma = SMOOTH_SIGMA_MS / 1000 / np.median(np.diff(time))\n        valid = likelihood > LIKELIHOOD_CUT            # x and y are NaN below this\n\n        # smooth and differentiate inside each run of tracked frames, never across a gap\n        for start, stop in _runs(valid):\n            if stop - start < 2:                       # a lone frame has no velocity\n                continue\n            sx = gaussian_filter1d(x[start:stop], sigma, mode='mirror')\n            sy = gaussian_filter1d(y[start:stop], sigma, mode='mirror')\n            speed[start:stop] = np.hypot(np.gradient(sx, time[start:stop]),\n                                         np.gradient(sy, time[start:stop]))\n        return time, speed\n\n    def _view_scale(self, velocity):\n        \"\"\"The percentile that puts one camera's velocity on a comparable scale.\"\"\"\n        pooled = np.concatenate([velocity[trial][1] for trial in self.trials])\n        return np.nanpercentile(pooled, NORMALIZE_PCT)\n\n    # the tongue is seen by both cameras, on different pixel scales, so each view is\n    # binned and divided by its own percentile before the two are averaged\n    def tongue_velocity(self):\n        \"\"\"Binned tongue velocity, as (n_trials, N_BINS).\"\"\"\n        side = np.array([_bin_frames(*self.side_tongue[trial]) for trial in self.trials])\n        bottom = np.array([_bin_frames(*self.bottom_tongue[trial]) for trial in self.trials])\n        side = side / self.side_scale\n        bottom = bottom / self.bottom_scale\n        return _mean_over_available(side, bottom)      # one view alone where the other is missing\n\n    # only one paw stays tracked through the delay, so it is used on its own, in pixels\n    def paw_velocity(self):\n        \"\"\"Binned paw velocity, as (n_trials, N_BINS).\"\"\"\n        return np.array([_bin_frames(*self.paw[trial]) for trial in self.trials])\n\n    # motion energy is already one number per frame, so it only needs binning\n    def motion_energy(self):\n        \"\"\"Binned motion energy, as (n_trials, N_BINS).\"\"\"\n        rows = []\n        for trial in self.trials:\n            time = self._frame_time(trial, SIDE)       # motion energy follows the side camera\n            inside = (time >= T_START) & (time <= T_STOP)\n            rows.append(_bin_frames(time[inside], self.energy[trial][inside]))\n        return np.array(rows)\n\n    def outputs(self):\n        \"\"\"The three streams, each cut in two at its own session median.\"\"\"\n        tongue = self.tongue_velocity()\n        paw = self.paw_velocity()\n        energy = self.motion_energy()\n        return (_discretize(tongue, np.nanpercentile(tongue, SPLIT_PCT)),\n                _discretize(paw, np.nanpercentile(paw, SPLIT_PCT)),\n                _discretize(energy, np.nanpercentile(energy, SPLIT_PCT)))\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------- neural --\n# Spike times are already on the behavior clock and relative to trial start, so aligning\n# to the go cue is one subtraction. Quality is a free text label written with either case\n# and with a few typos, so it is matched in lower case.\n\ndef _quality(cluster):\n    \"\"\"Quality label of a cluster; a handful carry no label at all.\"\"\"\n    label = cluster['quality']\n    return label.strip().lower() if isinstance(label, str) else ''\n\n\nclass Neural:\n    \"\"\"The QC-passing units of a session, as smoothed firing rates.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, obj, probes, trials):\n        self.obj = obj\n        self.go_cue = trial_column(obj['bp'], 'ev', 'goCue')\n        self.clusters = [cluster for probe in probes                # probes concatenated\n                         for cluster in self._probe_clusters(probe)\n                         if _quality(cluster) not in QUALITY_DROP]\n\n        # two sessions keep running after the recording stops, leaving trials with no spikes\n        last = max(int(np.asarray(cluster['trial'], int).max(initial=0))\n                   for cluster in self.clusters)\n        self.trials = np.asarray([t for t in trials if t < last])   # trial numbers are 1 based\n\n    def _probe_clusters(self, probe):\n        \"\"\"One dict per cluster on the given probe, however the file stores them.\"\"\"\n        entry = self.obj['clu'][probe - 1]\n        if isinstance(entry, dict) and isinstance(entry['tm'], list):\n            # v7.3: one entry per probe, each field holding one array per cluster\n            return [{field: entry[field][i] for field in entry}\n                    for i in range(len(entry['tm']))]\n        return self.obj['clu']                     # v5: one entry per cluster, single probe\n\n    def spike_count(self, cluster):\n        \"\"\"Spikes of one cluster per trial and bin, aligned to the go cue.\"\"\"\n        spike_trial = np.asarray(cluster['trial'], int) - 1         # trial numbers are 1 based\n        spike_time = np.asarray(cluster['trialtm'], float) - self.go_cue[spike_trial]\n\n        # count spikes into a trial by bin grid; those outside the window fall off the edges\n        trial_edges = np.arange(self.go_cue.size + 1) - 0.5\n        counts, _, _ = np.histogram2d(spike_trial, spike_time, bins=[trial_edges, BIN_EDGES])\n        return counts[self.trials]                                  # only the trials we keep\n\n    def rates(self):\n        \"\"\"Firing rate of every unit above MIN_RATE, as (n_units, n_trials, N_BINS).\"\"\"\n        sigma = RATE_SD_MS / 1000 / BIN\n        rates = []\n        for cluster in self.clusters:\n            rate = self.spike_count(cluster) / BIN                 # counts -> Hz\n            if rate.mean() > MIN_RATE:\n                rates.append(gaussian_filter1d(rate, sigma, axis=1, mode='reflect'))\n        return np.asarray(rates, np.float32)\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------ session --\n\ndef process_session(name, datadir):\n    \"\"\"Convert one session into per-trial neural, input, and output arrays.\"\"\"\n    obj = load_mat(session_file(name, 'data_structure', datadir))\n    info = trial_info(obj['bp'])\n\n    neural = Neural(obj, SESSIONS[name], info.index)\n    info = info.loc[neural.trials]                 # drops any trial past the recording\n    n_trials = len(info)\n\n    rates = neural.rates()\n    tongue, paw, energy = Camera(obj, name, info.index, datadir).outputs()\n\n    # the three per-trial values are repeated across bins so all six outputs share one array\n    out = np.empty((n_trials, len(OUTPUT_NAMES), N_BINS), np.int8)\n    out[:, 0, :] = info['lick_direction'].to_numpy()[:, None]\n    out[:, 1, :] = info['context'].to_numpy()[:, None]\n    out[:, 2, :] = info['outcome'].to_numpy()[:, None]\n    out[:, 3, :] = tongue\n    out[:, 4, :] = paw\n    out[:, 5, :] = energy\n\n    inp = np.tile(TIME.astype(np.float32), (n_trials, 1, 1))       # (n_trials, 1, N_BINS)\n\n    return {\n        'session_id': name,\n        'subject': name.split('_')[0],\n        'neural': [np.ascontiguousarray(rates[:, t, :]) for t in range(n_trials)],\n        'input': [inp[t] for t in range(n_trials)],\n        'output': [out[t] for t in range(n_trials)],\n        'n_trials': n_trials,\n        'n_units': int(rates.shape[0]),\n    }\n\n\ndef assemble(results):\n    \"\"\"Stitch per-session results into the target dictionary.\"\"\"\n    subjects = sorted({r['subject'] for r in results})\n    sub_ix = {s: i for i, s in enumerate(subjects)}\n\n    return {\n        'neural': [r['neural'] for r in results],\n        'input': [r['input'] for r in results],\n        'output': [r['output'] for r in results],\n\n        'subjects': subjects,\n        'subject_idx': np.array([sub_ix[r['subject']] for r in results], np.int32),\n\n        'brain_regions': [BRAIN_REGION],\n        'brain_region_idx': [np.zeros(r['n_units'], np.int32) for r in results],\n\n        'input_names': INPUT_NAMES,\n        'output_names': OUTPUT_NAMES,\n        'output_values': OUTPUT_VALUES,\n\n        'metadata': {\n            'task_description':\n                'Head-fixed mice lick left or right for water. In the delayed-response '\n                'context a tone instructs the direction, a delay follows, and an auditory '\n                'go cue opens the response window; in the water-cued context all cues are '\n                'omitted and water arrives at a random port. Decoded variables: lick '\n                'direction, behavioral context, outcome, and tongue, paw, and whole-body '\n                'movement.',\n            'time_bin_size': BIN * 1000.0,          # ms\n            'temporal_alignment_event': 'go cue onset (auditory chirp, 10 ms)',\n            'off_start': T_START,\n            'off_end': T_STOP,\n\n            'dataset': 'Hasnain, Birnbaum et al., Nat Neurosci 2024, ALM recordings',\n            'neural_units': 'Hz',\n            'session_selection':\n                \"The 44 sessions listed in the author's DataLoadingScripts/Recording and \"\n                'video/load<ANM>_ALMVideo.m that are present in data/, with the probe each '\n                'entry specifies; two-probe sessions are concatenated.',\n            'unit_curation':\n                'Cluster quality labels, lower cased, excluding %s, then units whose mean '\n                'rate over the window is at or below %.1f Hz.'\n                % (', '.join(sorted(QUALITY_DROP)), MIN_RATE),\n            'trial_curation': 'Early-lick and photostimulation trials excluded.',\n            'neural_processing':\n                'Spikes counted in %.0f ms bins, converted to Hz, smoothed with a gaussian '\n                'of %d ms standard deviation.' % (BIN * 1000, RATE_SD_MS),\n            'movement_discretisation':\n                'Tongue and paw velocity are the speed of the DeepLabCut position after a '\n                '%d ms gaussian, computed within each run of frames whose likelihood '\n                'exceeds %.1f. The two tongue views are each divided by their %dth '\n                'percentile before being averaged. Each stream is averaged into %.0f ms '\n                'bins and split at its own session %dth percentile; bins the camera did '\n                'not track take the trailing \"not visible\" class.'\n                % (SMOOTH_SIGMA_MS, LIKELIHOOD_CUT, NORMALIZE_PCT, BIN * 1000, SPLIT_PCT),\n            'session_info': [\n                {'session_id': r['session_id'], 'subject': r['subject'],\n                 'n_trials': r['n_trials'], 'n_units': r['n_units']}\n                for r in results\n            ],\n        },\n    }\n\n\ndef main():\n    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)\n    ap.add_argument('outfile', help='output pickle path')\n    ap.add_argument('--full', action='store_true', help='process all sessions (default)')\n    ap.add_argument('--sample', action='store_true', help='process only 2 sessions')\n    ap.add_argument('--datadir', default=DATA_DIR,\n                    help='directory holding the Ephys_Behavior/ folders (default: %(default)s)')\n    args = ap.parse_args()\n\n    names = SAMPLE if args.sample else list(SESSIONS)\n    print('%d sessions' % len(names), flush=True)\n\n    t0 = time.time()\n    results = []\n    for i, name in enumerate(names, 1):\n        t1 = time.time()\n        res = process_session(name, args.datadir)\n        results.append(res)\n        elapsed = time.time() - t0\n        print('[%2d/%d] %-18s %4d trials %4d units  %5.1fs  (elapsed %5.1fs, total ~%5.1fs)'\n              % (i, len(names), name, res['n_trials'], res['n_units'],\n                 time.time() - t1, elapsed, elapsed / i * len(names)), flush=True)\n\n    data = assemble(results)\n    print('\\nconverted in %.1f s' % (time.time() - t0), flush=True)\n    print('sessions %d | subjects %d | trials %d | units %d'\n          % (len(results), len(data['subjects']),\n             sum(r['n_trials'] for r in results), sum(r['n_units'] for r in results)),\n          flush=True)\n\n    with open(args.outfile, 'wb') as f:\n        pickle.dump(data, f, protocol=pickle.HIGHEST_PROTOCOL)\n    print('wrote %s (%.1f GB)'\n          % (args.outfile, os.path.getsize(args.outfile) / 1e9), flush=True)\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","numLines":571,"startLine":1,"totalLines":571}}}

  363→\n   364→def open_session(path):\n   365→    return _H5Session(path) if _is_hdf5(path) else _V7Session(path)\n   366→\n   367→\n   368→def load_motion_energy(path):\n   369→    \"\"\"motionEnergy_<anm>_<date>.mat -> list of per-trial 400 Hz traces.\n   370→\n   371→    Mirrors the layouts handled by loadMotionEnergy.m ('if isstruct(me.data)').\n   372→    All motion-energy files in this dataset are MATLAB v7 (MAT5).\n   373→    \"\"\"\n   374→    d = sio.loadmat(path, struct_as_record=False, squeeze_me=False)\n   375→    me = d['me']\n   376→    if isinstance(me, np.ndarray) and me.dtype == object and not hasattr(me[0, 0], '_fieldnames'):\n   377→        cells = me            # me is itself the cell array of trials\n   378→    else:\n   379→        me = me[0, 0]\n   380→        cells = me.data\n   381→        if hasattr(cells[0, 0], '_fieldnames'):   # me.data is a struct -> me.data.data\n   382→            cells = cells[0, 0].data\n   383→    return [np.asarray(x, float).ravel() for x in cells.ravel()]\n   384→\n   385→\n   386→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n   387→#  Signal processing helpers (ports of the reference MATLAB utilities)\n   388→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n   389→def gausswin(N, alpha=2.5):\n   390→    \"\"\"MATLAB gausswin(N, alpha)\"\"\"\n   391→    n = np.arange(N) - (N - 1) / 2.0\n   392→    return np.exp(-0.5 * (alpha * n / ((N - 1) / 2.0)) ** 2)\n   393→\n   394→\n   395→def _causal_kernel(N=SMOOTH_N):\n   396→    \"\"\"mySmooth.m: gaussian window, first floor(N/2) taps zeroed, normalised.\"\"\"\n   397→    k = gausswin(N)\n   398→    k[:N // 2] = 0.0\n   399→    k = k / k.sum()\n   400→    # conv(.,'same') with a kernel whose first N//2 taps are zero is the causal\n   401→    # FIR filter  y[i] = sum_j k[N//2 + j] * x[i - j].\n   402→    return k[N // 2:]\n   403→\n   404→\n   405→_FIR = _causal_kernel()\n   406→\n   407→\n   408→def my_smooth(x, N=SMOOTH_N, bctype=SMOOTH_BC):\n   409→    \"\"\"mySmooth(x, N, bctype) - operates on the first (time) dimension.\"\"\"\n   410→    if N <= 1:\n   411→        return x\n   412→    if bctype == 'reflect':\n   413→        xp = np.concatenate([x[:N], x], axis=0)\n   414→        trim = N\n   415→    elif bctype == 'zeropad':\n   416→        xp = np.concatenate([np.zeros((N,) + x.shape[1:], x.dtype), x], axis=0)\n   417→        trim = N\n   418→    else:\n   419→        xp, trim = x, 0\n   420→    y = lfilter(_FIR, [1.0], xp, axis=0)\n   421→    return y[trim:]\n   422→\n   423→\n   424→def interp_matlab(xq, xp, fp):\n   425→    \"\"\"interp1(xp, fp, xq) with MATLAB semantics: NaN outside the data range and\n   426→    NaN propagated from NaN samples.\"\"\"\n   427→    return np.interp(xq, xp, fp, left=np.nan, right=np.nan)\n   428→\n   429→\n   430→def nan_gradient(x):\n   431→    \"\"\"np.gradient-like first derivative that tolerates NaN gaps.\n   432→\n   433→    Central difference where both neighbours are finite, one-sided at the edges\n   434→    of a valid run, 0 for isolated samples. For gap-free signals this is exactly\n   435→    MATLAB's gradient() (unit spacing), which is what findVelocity.m uses.\n   436→    \"\"\"\n   437→    x = np.asarray(x, float)\n   438→    n = x.size\n   439→    fwd = np.full(n, np.nan)\n   440→    bwd = np.full(n, np.nan)\n   441→    fwd[:-1] = x[1:] - x[:-1]\n   442→    bwd[1:] = x[1:] - x[:-1]\n   443→    both = np.isfinite(fwd) & np.isfinite(bwd)\n   444→    g = np.where(both, (fwd + bwd) / 2.0,\n   445→                 np.where(np.isfinite(fwd), fwd,\n   446→                          np.where(np.isfinite(bwd), bwd, 0.0)))\n   447→    g[~np.isfinite(x)] = np.nan\n   448→    return g\n   449→\n   450→\n   451→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n   452→#  Per-session conversion\n   453→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n   454→def time_axis():\n   455→    \"\"\"getSeq.m: edges = tmin:dt:tmax, obj.time = edges + dt/2 (last dropped)\"\"\"\n   456→    edges = np.arange(TMIN, TMAX + DT / 2, DT)\n   457→    edges = edges[edges <= TMAX + 1e-9]\n   458→    t = edges + DT / 2\n   459→    return edges, t[:-1]\n   460→\n   461→\n   462→EDGES, TAXIS = time_axis()\n   463→NT = TAXIS.size\n   464→\n   465→\n   466→def bin_spikes(obj, probes, keep_clu, gocue, ntrials):\n   467→    \"\"\"Binned, smoothed single-trial firing rates.\n   468→\n   469→    Port of alignSpikes.m + getSeq.m: spike times are expressed relative to the\n   470→    alignment event and histogrammed into dt bins, divided by dt to give\n   471→    spikes/s, then smoothed with the causal gaussian kernel.\n   472→\n   473→    Returns (ntrials, NT, nunits) float32 array of firing rates.\n   474→    \"\"\"\n   475→    nunits = sum(len(k) for k in keep_clu.values())\n   476→    out = np.zeros((NT, nunits, ntrials), dtype=np.float32)\n   477→    col = 0\n   478→    for prb in probes:\n   479→        for i in keep_clu[prb]:\n   480→            tt, tr = obj.spikes(prb, i)\n   481→            ok = (tr >= 1) & (tr <= ntrials)\n   482→            tt, tr = tt[ok], tr[ok]\n   483→            al = tt - gocue[tr - 1]                      # trialtm_aligned\n   484→            b = np.floor((al - TMIN) / DT).astype(np.int64)\n   485→            m = (b >= 0) & (b < NT)\n   486→            if m.any():\n   487→                idx = (tr[m] - 1) * NT + b[m]\n   488→                cnt = np.bincount(idx, minlength=ntrials * NT)\n   489→                out[:, col, :] = cnt.reshape(ntrials, NT).T.astype(np.float32)\n   490→            col += 1\n   491→    out /= DT                                            # spikes / s\n   492→    sm = my_smooth(out.reshape(NT, -1)).astype(np.float32)\n   493→    return sm.reshape(NT, nunits, ntrials)\n   494→\n   495→\n   496→def mean_firing_rates(obj, probes, keep_clu, gocue, ntrials):\n   497→    \"\"\"Mean FR (Hz) over the aligned analysis window, across all trials.\n   498→\n   499→    removeLowFRClusters.m: \"all those firing less than lowFR spikes per second\n   500→    on average across all trials\".\n   501→    \"\"\"\n   502→    frs = []\n   503→    for prb in probes:\n   504→        for i in keep_clu[prb]:\n   505→            tt, tr = obj.spikes(prb, i)\n   506→            ok = (tr >= 1) & (tr <= ntrials)\n   507→            al = tt[ok] - gocue[tr[ok] - 1]\n   508→            n = np.count_nonzero((al >= TMIN) & (al < TMAX))\n   509→            frs.append(n / ((TMAX - TMIN) * ntrials))\n   510→    return np.array(frs)\n   511→\n   512→\n   513→def video_offset(obj):\n   514→    \"\"\"findVideoOffset.m\"\"\"\n   515→    bitstart = obj.sglx_bitstart()\n   516→    fs = obj.sglx_fs()\n   517→    bp_bitstart = obj.bp('ev.bitStart')\n   518→    bp_bitstart = bp_bitstart[np.isfinite(bp_bitstart)]\n   519→    return (stats.mode(bitstart, keepdims=False).mode / fs\n   520→            - stats.mode(bp_bitstart, keepdims=False).mode)\n   521→\n   522→\n   523→def kinematic_speed(obj, view, featname, gocue, ntrials, vidshift):\n   524→    \"\"\"Interpolated DLC speed for one feature, aligned to the go cue.\n   525→\n   526→    Returns\n   527→        speed   (ntrials, NT) float, |d(position)/dt| in px/bin\n   528→        visible (ntrials, NT) bool, True where DLC tracked the feature\n   529→    Mirrors findPosition.m / findVelocity.m: interpolate the raw DLC trace onto\n   530→    the neural time axis (video clock shifted by findVideoOffset and the\n   531→    alignment event), then differentiate.\n   532→    \"\"\"\n   533→    feats = obj.featnames(view)\n   534→    featix = feats.index(featname)\n   535→    speed = np.full((ntrials, NT), np.nan)\n   536→    visible = np.zeros((ntrials, NT), dtype=bool)\n   537→    taxis = TAXIS + ADVANCE_MOVEMENT\n   538→    for t in range(ntrials):\n   539→        xy, ft = obj.traj_xy(view, t, featix)\n   540→        if xy is None or not np.isfinite(obj.ndropped(view, t)):\n   541→            continue                                   # no usable video\n   542→        # findPosition.m: if frameTimes is missing/all NaN, fall back to a\n   543→        # nominal 400 Hz frame clock\n   544→        if ft is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(ft)) or ft.size != xy.shape[1]:\n   545→            ft = np.arange(1, xy.shape[1] + 1) / 400.0 - 0.5 + vidshift\n   546→        tv = ft - vidshift - gocue[t]\n   547→        x = interp_matlab(taxis, tv, xy[0])\n   548→        y = interp_matlab(taxis, tv, xy[1])\n   549→        vis = np.isfinite(x) & np.isfinite(y)\n   550→        if not vis.any():\n   551→            continue\n   552→        vx = nan_gradient(x)\n   553→        vy = nan_gradient(y)\n   554→        speed[t] = np.sqrt(vx ** 2 + vy ** 2)\n   555→        visible[t] = vis & np.isfinite(speed[t])\n   556→    return speed, visible\n   557→\n   558→\n   559→def motion_energy_trace(obj, me_cells, gocue, ntrials, vidshift):\n   560→    \"\"\"loadMotionEnergy.m: interpolate 400 Hz motion energy onto the neural axis.\"\"\"\n   561→    out = np.full((ntrials, NT), np.nan)\n   562→    taxis = TAXIS + ADVANCE_MOVEMENT\n   563→    for t in range(min(ntrials, len(me_cells))):\n   564→        m = me_cells[t]\n   565→        if m.size == 0:\n   566→            continue\n   567→        ft = obj.frame_times(0, t)\n   568→        if ft is None or ft.size != m.size or not np.all(np.isfinite(ft)):\n   569→            ft = (np.arange(1, m.size + 1) / 400.0) - 0.5 + vidshift  # fallback\n   570→        out[t] = interp_matlab(taxis, ft - vidshift - gocue[t], m)\n   571→    return out\n   572→\n   573→\n   574→def discretize(values, valid, thresh=None):\n   575→    \"\"\"0 = < median, 1 = >= median, 2 = invalid (not visible / no video).\"\"\"\n   576→    v = np.full(values.shape, 2, dtype=np.int8)\n   577→    if thresh is None:\n   578→        thresh = np.nanpercentile(values[valid], 50) if valid.any() else np.nan\n   579→    if valid.any():\n   580→        v[valid] = (values[valid] >= thresh).astype(np.int8)\n   581→    return v, thresh\n   582→\n   583→\n   584→def process_session(anm, date, probes, datadir, task, show=False, outdir='/app'):\n   585→    \"\"\"Load + process one session. Returns a dict of converted arrays.\"\"\"\n   586→    t0 = time.time()\n   587→    fn = os.path.join(datadir, f'data_structure_{anm}_{date}.mat')\n   588→    obj = open_session(fn)\n   589→    timing = {}\n   590→\n   591→    ntrials = obj.Ntrials\n   592→    gocue = obj.bp('ev.goCue')\n   593→    sample = obj.bp('ev.sample')\n   594→    delay = obj.bp('ev.delay')\n   595→    hit, miss, no = obj.bp('hit'), obj.bp('miss'), obj.bp('no')\n   596→    R, L = obj.bp('R'), obj.bp('L')\n   597→    autowater = obj.bp('autowater')\n   598→    early = obj.bp('early')\n   599→    stim = obj.bp('stim.enable') if obj.has_bp('stim.enable') else np.zeros(ntrials)\n   600→\n   601→    # ---- cluster curation (findClusters.m, case-insensitive) --------------\n   602→    keep_clu, qualities = {}, {}\n   603→    for prb in probes:\n   604→        q = obj.qualities(prb)\n   605→        qualities[prb] = q\n   606→        keep_clu[prb] = [i for i, qq in enumerate(q) if qq.lower() not in BAD_QUALITY]\n   607→    n_quality = sum(len(v) for v in keep_clu.values())\n   608→\n   609→    # ---- low firing rate removal (removeLowFRClusters.m) ------------------\n   610→    fr = mean_firing_rates(obj, probes, keep_clu, gocue, ntrials)\n   611→    use = fr > LOW_FR\n   612→    off = 0\n   613→    for prb in probes:\n   614→        k = len(keep_clu[prb])\n   615→        keep_clu[prb] = [c for c, u in zip(keep_clu[prb], use[off:off + k]) if u]\n   616→        off += k\n   617→    nunits = sum(len(v) for v in keep_clu.values())\n   618→    timing['curation'] = time.time() - t0\n   619→\n   620→    # per-unit metadata\n   621→    unit_quality, unit_region, unit_probe = [], [], []\n   622→    for prb in probes:\n   623→        loc = obj.probe_loc(prb)\n   624→        region = 'tjM1' if 'M1TJ' in loc.upper() else 'ALM'\n   625→        for i in keep_clu[prb]:\n   626→            unit_quality.append(qualities[prb][i].lower())\n   627→            unit_region.append(region)\n   628→            unit_probe.append((prb, loc))\n   629→\n   630→    # ---- neural: binned, smoothed single trial firing rates ---------------\n   631→    t1 = time.time()\n   632→    trialdat = bin_spikes(obj, probes, keep_clu, gocue, ntrials)   # (NT, nunits, ntrials)\n   633→    timing['neural'] = time.time() - t1\n   634→\n   635→    # ---- video derived outputs -------------------------------------------\n   636→    t1 = time.time()\n   637→    vidshift = video_offset(obj)\n   638→    tongue_speed, tongue_vis = kinematic_speed(obj, TONGUE_VIEW, TONGUE_FEAT,\n   639→                                               gocue, ntrials, vidshift)\n   640→    paw_speed, paw_vis = kinematic_speed(obj, PAW_VIEW, PAW_FEAT,\n   641→                                         gocue, ntrials, vidshift)\n   642→    mefn = os.path.join(datadir, f'motionEnergy_{anm}_{date}.mat')\n   643→    me_cells = load_motion_energy(mefn)\n   644→    me = motion_energy_trace(obj, me_cells, gocue, ntrials, vidshift)\n   645→    me_vis = np.isfinite(me)\n   646→    timing['video'] = time.time() - t1\n   647→\n   648→    # ---- trial curation ---------------------------------------------------\n   649→    # early-lick and photoinactivation trials are excluded from all analyses in\n   650→    # the reference (params.condition: '~stim.enable & ~early'; Methods:\n   651→    # \"Trials in which the animal contacted the lickport before the reward\n   652→    # ('early lick') were omitted from analyses\").\n   653→    keep = (early < 0.5) & (stim < 0.5) & np.isfinite(gocue)\n   654→    # trials with no spikes at all on any unit = no ephys coverage (the\n   655→    # recording ended before the behavioural session did)\n   656→    spk_per_trial = trialdat.sum(axis=(0, 1))\n   657→    no_ephys = spk_per_trial <= 0\n   658→    keep &= ~no_ephys\n   659→    ntrials_keep = int(keep.sum())\n   660→\n   661→    # ---- outputs ----------------------------------------------------------\n   662→    # lick direction (getPrevChoice.m: choice = (R&hit) | (L&miss))\n   663→    lickdir = np.full(ntrials, 2, dtype=np.int8)                 # none\n   664→    lickdir[((R > 0.5) & (hit > 0.5)) | ((L > 0.5) & (miss > 0.5))] = 1   # right\n   665→    lickdir[((L > 0.5) & (hit > 0.5)) | ((R > 0.5) & (miss > 0.5))] = 0   # left\n   666→    lickdir[no > 0.5] = 2\n   667→\n   668→    context = np.where(autowater > 0.5, 0, 1).astype(np.int8)    # 0=WC, 1=DR\n   669→\n   670→    outcome = np.full(ntrials, -1, dtype=np.int8)\n   671→    outcome[miss > 0.5] = 0                                      # incorrect\n   672→    outcome[hit > 0.5] = 1                                       # correct\n   673→    outcome[no > 0.5] = 2                                        # ignore\n   674→\n   675→    # per-session median thresholds, computed on the retained trials only\n   676→    tng_d, tng_thr = discretize(tongue_speed[keep], tongue_vis[keep])\n   677→    paw_d, paw_thr = discretize(paw_speed[keep], paw_vis[keep])\n   678→    me_d, me_thr = discretize(me[keep], me_vis[keep])\n   679→\n   680→    kt = np.flatnonzero(keep)\n   681→    neural, inputs, outputs = [], [], []\n   682→    inp = TAXIS.astype(np.float32)[None, :]\n   683→    for j, t in enumerate(kt):\n   684→        neural.append(np.ascontiguousarray(trialdat[:, :, t].T))       # (nunits, NT)\n   685→        inputs.append(inp.copy())\n   686→        out = np.empty((6, NT), dtype=np.int8)\n   687→        out[0] = lickdir[t]\n   688→        out[1] = context[t]\n   689→        out[2] = outcome[t]\n   690→        out[3] = tng_d[j]\n   691→        out[4] = paw_d[j]\n   692→        out[5] = me_d[j]\n   693→        outputs.append(out)\n   694→\n   695→    info = dict(\n   696→        animal=anm, date=date, task=task, probes=[int(p) for p in probes],\n   697→        probe_locations=sorted(set(p[1] for p in unit_probe)),\n   698→        file_format=obj.fmt,\n   699→        ntrials_total=int(ntrials), ntrials_kept=ntrials_keep,\n   700→        n_early=int(np.sum(early > 0.5)), n_stim=int(np.sum(stim > 0.5)),\n   701→        n_no_ephys=int(np.sum(no_ephys)),\n   702→        nunits_all=sum(len(qualities[p]) for p in probes),\n   703→        nunits_quality=n_quality, nunits=nunits,\n   704→        # provenance: 0-based indices of the retained trials and of the retained\n   705→        # clusters (index into obj.clu{probe}) in the original data object\n   706→        trial_indices=np.flatnonzero(keep).astype(np.int32),\n   707→        cluster_indices={int(p): np.asarray(keep_clu[p], dtype=np.int32) for p in probes},\n   708→        unit_quality=list(unit_quality),\n   709→        n_single_units=int(sum(q in SINGLE_UNIT_QUALITY for q in unit_quality)),\n   710→        video_offset_s=float(vidshift),\n   711→        median_delay_s=float(np.nanmedian(gocue - delay)),\n   712→        median_sample_s=float(np.nanmedian(delay - sample)),\n   713→        tongue_speed_threshold=float(tng_thr), paw_speed_threshold=float(paw_thr),\n   714→        motion_energy_threshold=float(me_thr),\n   715→        frac_context_WC=float(np.mean(context[keep] == 0)),\n   716→        frac_outcome=[float(np.mean(outcome[keep] == c)) for c in range(3)],\n   717→        frac_lickdir=[float(np.mean(lickdir[keep] == c)) for c in range(3)],\n   718→        frac_tongue_notvisible=float(np.mean(tng_d == 2)),\n   719→        frac_paw_notvisible=float(np.mean(paw_d == 2)),\n   720→        frac_me_novideo=float(np.mean(me_d == 2)),\n   721→        mean_firing_rate=float(np.mean([f for f, u in zip(fr, use) if u])) if nunits else 0.0,\n   722→    )\n   723→\n   724→    if outcome[keep].min() < 0:\n   725→        raise RuntimeError(f'{anm} {date}: trial with no hit/miss/no outcome')\n   726→\n   727→    res = dict(neural=neural, input=inputs, output=outputs,\n   728→               region=unit_region, quality=unit_quality, info=info,\n   729→               subject=anm)\n   730→\n   731→    if show:\n   732→        _plot_processing(outdir, anm, date, obj, gocue, sample, delay, keep, kt,\n   733→                         trialdat, tongue_speed, tongue_vis, tng_thr,\n   734→                         paw_speed, paw_vis, paw_thr, me, me_vis, me_thr,\n   735→                         lickdir, context, outcome, unit_region)\n   736→\n   737→    timing['total'] = time.time() - t0\n   738→    info['timing'] = {k: round(v, 2) for k, v in timing.items()}\n   739→    obj.close()\n   740→    return res\n   741→\n   742→\n   743→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n   744→#  Diagnostic plots\n   745→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n   746→def _plot_processing(outdir, anm, date, obj, gocue, sample, delay, keep, kt,\n   747→                     trialdat, tongue_speed, tongue_vis, tng_thr,\n   748→                     paw_speed, paw_vis, paw_thr, me, me_vis, me_thr,\n   749→                     lickdir, context, outcome, unit_region):\n   750→    import matplotlib\n   751→    matplotlib.use('Agg')\n   752→    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n   753→\n   754→    ev_sample = np.median(sample - gocue)\n   755→    ev_delay = np.median(delay - gocue)\n   756→    fig, ax = plt.subplots(4, 2, figsize=(18, 20))\n   757→\n   758→    # (1) raster + smoothed rate for the most active unit, first kept trials\n   759→    rates = trialdat[:, :, keep].mean(axis=(0, 2))\n   760→    u = int(np.argmax(rates))\n   761→    axx = ax[0, 0]\n   762→    ntr = min(40, len(kt))\n   763→    lL, lR = obj.licks('L'), obj.licks('R')\n   764→    for j, t in enumerate(kt[:ntr]):\n   765→        axx.plot(TAXIS, trialdat[:, u, t] / max(1e-9, trialdat[:, u, kt[:ntr]].max()) * 0.9 + j,\n   766→                 lw=0.5, color='k')\n   767→        for lt, c in ((lL[t], 'r'), (lR[t], 'b')):\n   768→            lt = np.asarray(lt).ravel() - gocue[t]\n   769→            lt = lt[(lt > TMIN) & (lt < TMAX)]\n   770→            axx.plot(lt, np.full(lt.size, j + 0.5), '.', color=c, ms=2)\n   771→    for e, c in ((0, 'k'), (ev_sample, 'g'), (ev_delay, 'm')):\n   772→        axx.axvline(e, color=c, ls='--', lw=1)\n   773→    axx.set_title(f'{anm} {date}: unit {u} smoothed rate per trial (black)\\n'\n   774→                  'licks: red=left blue=right; dashed: sample/delay/go cue')\n   775→    axx.set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)'); axx.set_ylabel('trial')\n   776→\n   777→    # (2) population PSTH split by lick direction\n   778→    axx = ax[0, 1]\n   779→    for d, c, lab in ((0, 'r', 'left'), (1, 'b', 'right'), (2, 'gray', 'none')):\n   780→        m = keep & (lickdir == d)\n   781→        if m.sum() > 2:\n   782→            axx.plot(TAXIS, trialdat[:, :, m].mean(axis=(1, 2)), color=c, label=lab)\n   783→    for e, c in ((0, 'k'), (ev_sample, 'g'), (ev_delay, 'm')):\n   784→        axx.axvline(e, color=c, ls='--', lw=1)\n   785→    axx.legend(); axx.set_title('population mean firing rate by lick direction')\n   786→    axx.set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)'); axx.set_ylabel('spk/s')\n   787→\n   788→    # (3) tongue speed + discretisation\n   789→    for row, (sp, vis, thr, name) in enumerate(\n   790→            [(tongue_speed, tongue_vis, tng_thr, 'tongue speed'),\n   791→             (paw_speed, paw_vis, paw_thr, 'paw speed'),\n   792→             (me, me_vis, me_thr, 'motion energy')]):\n   793→        axx = ax[row + 1, 0]\n   794→        for j, t in enumerate(kt[:6]):\n   795→            v = sp[t].copy()\n   796→            axx.plot(TAXIS, v + j * 3 * (thr if np.isfinite(thr) else 1), 'k', lw=0.8)\n   797→            iv = ~vis[t]\n   798→            axx.plot(TAXIS[iv], np.full(iv.sum(), j * 3 * thr), 'r.', ms=2)\n   799→            axx.axhline(j * 3 * thr + thr, color='b', lw=0.5)\n   800→        axx.axvline(0, color='k', ls='--')\n   801→        axx.set_title(f'{name}: traces for 6 trials (blue = per-session median '\n   802→                      f'{thr:.3g}, red = invalid)')\n   803→        axx.set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)')\n   804→\n   805→        axx = ax[row + 1, 1]\n   806→        vals = sp[keep][vis[keep]]\n   807→        axx.hist(vals, bins=100)\n   808→        axx.axvline(thr, color='b')\n   809→        axx.set_yscale('log')\n   810→        axx.set_title(f'{name} distribution over valid samples; median={thr:.3g}; '\n   811→                      f'frac invalid={1 - np.mean(vis[keep]):.3f}')\n   812→\n   813→    fig.tight_layout()\n   814→    fn = os.path.join(outdir, f'processing_{anm}_{date}.png')\n   815→    fig.savefig(fn, dpi=110)\n   816→    plt.close(fig)\n   817→    print(f'  wrote {fn}')\n   818→\n   819→\n   820→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n   821→#  Main\n   822→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n   823→def main():\n   824→    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)\n   825→    ap.add_argument('outfile')\n   826→    ap.add_argument('--full', action='store_true', help='process all sessions (default)')\n   827→    ap.add_argument('--sample', action='store_true', help='process only 2 sessions')\n   828→    ap.add_argument('--show-processing', action='store_true',\n   829→                    help='save per-step diagnostic plots for up to 2 sessions')\n   830→    args = ap.parse_args()\n   831→\n   832→    sessions = SESSIONS\n   833→    if args.sample:\n   834→        # one fixed-delay two-context session and one randomized-delay session\n   835→        sessions = [SESSIONS[2], SESSIONS[29]]\n   836→\n   837→    t_start = time.time()\n   838→    data = dict(neural=[], input=[], output=[], subjects=[], subject_idx=[],\n   839→                brain_regions=[], brain_region_idx=[],\n   840→                input_names=INPUT_NAMES, output_names=OUTPUT_NAMES,\n   841→                output_values=OUTPUT_VALUES, metadata={})\n   842→    subjects, regions, session_info = [], [], []\n   843→    nshown = 0\n   844→    for k, (anm, date, probes, datadir, task) in enumerate(sessions):\n   845→        show = args.show_processing and nshown < 2\n   846→        res = process_session(anm, date, probes, datadir, task, show=show,\n   847→                              outdir=os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(args.outfile)) or '.')\n   848→        nshown += int(show)\n   849→        if anm not in subjects:\n   850→            subjects.append(anm)\n   851→        for r in res['region']:\n   852→            if r not in regions:\n   853→                regions.append(r)\n   854→        data['neural'].append(res['neural'])\n   855→        data['input'].append(res['input'])\n   856→        data['output'].append(res['output'])\n   857→        data['subject_idx'].append(subjects.index(anm))\n   858→        data['brain_region_idx'].append(np.array([regions.index(r) for r in res['region']],\n   859→                                                 dtype=np.int64))\n   860→        session_info.append(res['info'])\n   861→        i = res['info']\n   862→        print(f\"[{k + 1}/{len(sessions)}] {anm} {date} ({i['file_format']}) \"\n   863→              f\"units {i['nunits_all']}->{i['nunits_quality']}->{i['nunits']} \"\n   864→              f\"(SU {i['n_single_units']}) | trials {i['ntrials_total']}->{i['ntrials_kept']} \"\n   865→              f\"(early {i['n_early']}, stim {i['n_stim']}, no-ephys {i['n_no_ephys']}) | \"\n   866→              f\"WC {i['frac_context_WC']:.2f} | invalid tongue/paw/me \"\n   867→              f\"{i['frac_tongue_notvisible']:.2f}/{i['frac_paw_notvisible']:.2f}/\"\n   868→              f\"{i['frac_me_novideo']:.2f} | {i['timing']['total']:.1f}s \"\n   869→              f\"{i['timing']}\", flush=True)\n   870→\n   871→    data['subjects'] = subjects\n   872→    data['subject_idx'] = np.array(data['subject_idx'], dtype=np.int64)\n   873→    data['brain_regions'] = regions\n   874→    data['metadata'] = dict(\n   875→        task_description=(\n   876→            'Head-fixed mice performed directional licking tasks. In delayed-response '\n   877→            '(DR) trials an auditory tone (1.3 s sample epoch) instructed a left or right '\n   878→            'lick, which had to be withheld through a delay epoch (0.9 s fixed, or '\n   879→            'randomized 0.3-3.6 s in the randomized-delay sessions) until an auditory go '\n   880→            'cue; correct licks were rewarded with water. In water-cued (WC) trials, '\n   881→            'interleaved in blocks of 10-25 trials in a subset of sessions, all auditory '\n   882→            'cues were omitted and water was delivered at a random port at a random time; '\n   883→            'mice had to infer the context. Neural activity was recorded extracellularly '\n   884→            'with silicon probes in anterior lateral motor cortex (ALM; a few probes were '\n   885→            'located in tongue-jaw motor cortex, tjM1). processing helpers (ports of the reference MATLAB utilities)\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\ndef gausswin(N, alpha=2.5):\n    \"\"\"MATLAB gausswin(N, alpha)\"\"\"\n    n = np.arange(N) - (N - 1) / 2.0\n    return np.exp(-0.5 * (alpha * n / ((N - 1) / 2.0)) ** 2)\n\n\ndef _causal_kernel(N=SMOOTH_N):\n    \"\"\"mySmooth.m: gaussian window, first floor(N/2) taps zeroed, normalised.\"\"\"\n    k = gausswin(N)\n    k[:N // 2] = 0.0\n    k = k / k.sum()\n    # conv(.,'same') with a kernel whose first N//2 taps are zero is the causal\n    # FIR filter  y[i] = sum_j k[N//2 + j] * x[i - j].\n    return k[N // 2:]\n\n\n_FIR = _causal_kernel()\n\n\ndef my_smooth(x, N=SMOOTH_N, bctype=SMOOTH_BC):\n    \"\"\"mySmooth(x, N, bctype) - operates on the first (time) dimension.\"\"\"\n    if N <= 1:\n        return x\n    if bctype == 'reflect':\n        xp = np.concatenate([x[:N], x], axis=0)\n        trim = N\n    elif bctype == 'zeropad':\n        xp = np.concatenate([np.zeros((N,) + x.shape[1:], x.dtype), x], axis=0)\n        trim = N\n    else:\n        xp, trim = x, 0\n    y = lfilter(_FIR, [1.0], xp, axis=0)\n    return y[trim:]\n\n\ndef left=np.nan, right=np.nan)\n\n\ndef nan_gradient(x):\n    \"\"\"np.gradient-like first derivative that tolerates NaN gaps.\n\n    Central difference where both neighbours are finite, one-sided at the edges\n    of a valid run, 0 for isolated samples. For gap-free signals this is exactly\n    MATLAB's gradient() (unit spacing), which is what findVelocity.m uses.\n    \"\"\"\n    x = np.asarray(x, float)\n    n = x.size\n    fwd = np.full(n, np.nan)\n    bwd = np.full(n, np.nan)\n    fwd[:-1] = x[1:] - x[:-1]\n    bwd[1:] = x[1:] - x[:-1]\n    both = np.isfinite(fwd) & np.isfinite(bwd)\n    g = np.where(both, (fwd + bwd) / 2.0,\n                 np.where(np.isfinite(fwd), fwd,\n                          np.where(np.isfinite(bwd), bwd, 0.0)))\n    g[~np.isfinite(x)] = np.nan\n    return g\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n#  Per-session conversion\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\ndef time_axis():\n    \"\"\"getSeq.m: edges = tmin:dt:tmax, obj.time = edges + dt/2 (last dropped)\"\"\"\n    edges = np.arange(TMIN, TMAX + DT / 2, DT)\n    edges = edges[edges <= TMAX + 1e-9]\n    t = edges + DT / 2\n    return edges, t[:-1]\n\n\nEDGES, TAXIS = time_axis()\nNT = TAXIS.size\n\n\ndef bin_spikes(obj, probes, keep_clu, gocue, ntrials):\n    \"\"\"Binned, smoothed single-trial firing rates.\n\n    Port of alignSpikes.m + getSeq.m: spike times are expressed relative to the\n    alignment event and histogrammed into dt bins, divided by dt to give\n    spikes/s, then smoothed with the causal gaussian kernel.\n\n    Returns (ntrials, NT, nunits) float32 array of firing rates.\n    \"\"\"\n    nunits = sum(len(k) for k in keep_clu.values())\n    out = np.zeros((NT, nunits, ntrials), dtype=np.float32)\n    col = 0\n    for prb in probes:\n        for i in keep_clu[prb]:\n            tt, tr = obj.spikes(prb, i)\n            ok = (tr >= 1) & (tr <= ntrials)\n            tt, tr = tt[ok], tr[ok]\n            al = tt - gocue[tr - 1]                      # trialtm_aligned\n            b = np.floor((al - TMIN) / DT).astype(np.int64)\n            m = (b >= 0) & (b < NT)\n            if m.any():\n                idx = (tr[m] - 1) * NT + b[m]\n                cnt = np.bincount(idx, minlength=ntrials * NT)\n                out[:, col, :] = cnt.reshape(ntrials, NT).T.astype(np.float32)\n            col += 1\n    out /= DT                                            # spikes / s\n    sm = my_smooth(out.reshape(NT, -1)).astype(np.float32)\n    return sm.reshape(NT, nunits, ntrials)\n\n\ndef mean_firing_rates(obj, probes, keep_clu, gocue, ntrials):\n    \"\"\"Mean FR (Hz) over the aligned analysis window, across all trials.\n\n    removeLowFRClusters.m: \"all those firing less than lowFR spikes per second\n    on average across all trials\".\n    \"\"\"\n    frs = []\n    for prb in probes:\n        for i in keep_clu[prb]:\n            tt, tr = obj.spikes(prb, i)\n            ok = (tr >= 1) & (tr <= ntrials)\n            al = tt[ok] - gocue[tr[ok] - 1]\n            n = np.count_nonzero((al >= TMIN) & (al < TMAX))\n            frs.append(n / ((TMAX - TMIN) * ntrials))\n    return np.array(frs)\n\n\ndef video_offset(obj):\n    \"\"\"findVideoOffset.m\"\"\"\n    bitstart = obj.sglx_bitstart()\n    fs = obj.sglx_fs()\n    bp_bitstart = obj.bp('ev.bitStart')\n    bp_bitstart = bp_bitstart[np.isfinite(bp_bitstart)]\n    return (stats.mode(bitstart, keepdims=False).mode / fs\n            - stats.mode(bp_bitstart, keepdims=False).mode)\n\n\ndef kinematic_speed(obj, view, featname, gocue, ntrials, vidshift):\n    \"\"\"Interpolated DLC speed for one feature, aligned to the go cue.\n\n    Returns\n        speed   (ntrials, NT) float, |d(position)/dt| in px/bin\n        visible (ntrials, NT) bool, True where DLC tracked the feature\n    Mirrors findPosition.m / findVelocity.m: interpolate the raw DLC trace onto\n    the neural time axis (video clock shifted by findVideoOffset and the\n    alignment event), then differentiate.\n    \"\"\"\n    feats = obj.featnames(view)\n    featix = feats.index(featname)\n    speed = np.full((ntrials, NT), np.nan)\n    visible = np.zeros((ntrials, NT), dtype=bool)\n    taxis = TAXIS + ADVANCE_MOVEMENT\n    for t in range(ntrials):\n        xy, ft = obj.traj_xy(view, t, featix)\n        if xy is None or not np.isfinite(obj.ndropped(view, t)):\n            continue                                   # no usable video\n        # findPosition.m: if frameTimes is missing/all NaN, fall back to a\n        # nominal 400 Hz frame clock\n        if ft is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(ft)) or ft.size != xy.shape[1]:\n            ft = np.arange(1, xy.shape[1] + 1) / 400.0 - 0.5 + vidshift\n        tv = ft - vidshift - gocue[t]\n        x = interp_matlab(taxis, tv, xy[0])\n        y = interp_matlab(taxis, tv, xy[1])\n        vis = np.isfinite(x) & np.isfinite(y)\n        if not vis.any():\n            continue\n        vx = nan_gradient(x)\n        vy = nan_gradient(y)\n        speed[t] = np.sqrt(vx ** 2 + vy ** 2)\n        visible[t] = vis & np.isfinite(speed[t])\n    return speed, visible\n\n\ndef motion_energy_trace(obj, me_cells, gocue, ntrials, vidshift):\n    \"\"\"loadMotionEnergy.m: interpolate 400 Hz motion energy onto the neural axis.\"\"\"\n    out = np.full((ntrials, NT), np.nan)\n    taxis = TAXIS + ADVANCE_MOVEMENT\n    for t in range(min(ntrials, len(me_cells))):\n        m = me_cells[t]\n        if m.size == 0:\n            continue\n        ft = obj.frame_times(0, t)\n        if ft is None or ft.size != m.size or not np.all(np.isfinite(ft)):\n            ft = (np.arange(1, m.size + 1) / 400.0) - 0.5 + vidshift  # fallback\n        out[t] = interp_matlab(taxis, ft - vidshift - gocue[t], m)\n    return out\n\n\ndef discretize(values, valid, thresh=None):\n    \"\"\"0 = < median, 1 = >= median, 2 = invalid (not visible / no video).\"\"\"\n    v = np.full(values.shape, 2, dtype=np.int8)\n    if thresh is None:\n        thresh = np.nanpercentile(values[valid], 50) if valid.any() else np.nan\n    if valid.any():\n        v[valid] = (values[valid] >= thresh).astype(np.int8)\n    return v, thresh\n\n\ndef process_session(anm, date, probes, datadir, task, show=False, outdir='/app'):\n    \"\"\"Load + process one session. Returns a dict of converted arrays.\"\"\"\n    t0 = time.time()\n    fn = os.path.join(datadir, f'data_structure_{anm}_{date}.mat')\n    obj = open_session(fn)\n    timing = {}\n\n    ntrials = obj.Ntrials\n    gocue = obj.bp('ev.goCue')\n    sample = obj.bp('ev.sample')\n    delay = obj.bp('ev.delay')\n    hit, miss, no = obj.bp('hit'), obj.bp('miss'), obj.bp('no')\n    R, L = obj.bp('R'), obj.bp('L')\n    autowater = obj.bp('autowater')\n    early = obj.bp('early')\n    stim = obj.bp('stim.enable') if obj.has_bp('stim.enable') else np.zeros(ntrials)\n\n    # ---- cluster curation (findClusters.m, case-insensitive) --------------\n    keep_clu, qualities = {}, {}\n    for prb in probes:\n        q = obj.qualities(prb)\n        qualities[prb] = q\n        keep_clu[prb] = [i for i, qq in enumerate(q) if qq.lower() not in BAD_QUALITY]\n    n_quality = sum(len(v) for v in keep_clu.values())\n\n    # ---- low firing rate removal (removeLowFRClusters.m) ------------------\n    fr = mean_firing_rates(obj, probes, keep_clu, gocue, ntrials)\n    use = fr > LOW_FR\n    off = 0\n    for prb in probes:\n        k = len(keep_clu[prb])\n        keep_clu[prb] = [c for c, u in zip(keep_clu[prb], use[off:off + k]) if u]\n        off += k\n    nunits = sum(len(v) for v in keep_clu.values())\n    timing['curation'] = time.time() - t0\n\n    # per-unit metadata\n    unit_quality, unit_region, unit_probe = [], [], []\n    for prb in probes:\n        loc = obj.probe_loc(prb)\n        region = 'tjM1' if 'M1TJ' in loc.upper() else 'ALM'\n        for i in keep_clu[prb]:\n            unit_quality.append(qualities[prb][i].lower())\n            unit_region.append(region)\n            unit_probe.append((prb, loc))\n\n    # ---- neural: binned, smoothed single trial firing rates ---------------\n    t1 = time.time()\n    trialdat = bin_spikes(obj, probes, keep_clu, gocue, ntrials)   # (NT, nunits, ntrials)\n    timing['neural'] = time.time() - t1\n\n    # ---- video derived outputs -------------------------------------------\n    t1 = time.time()\n    vidshift = video_offset(obj)\n    tongue_speed, tongue_vis = kinematic_speed(obj, TONGUE_VIEW, TONGUE_FEAT,\n                                               gocue, ntrials, vidshift)\n    paw_speed, paw_vis = kinematic_speed(obj, PAW_VIEW, PAW_FEAT,\n                                         gocue, ntrials, vidshift)\n    mefn = os.path.join(datadir, f'motionEnergy_{anm}_{date}.mat')\n    me_cells = load_motion_energy(mefn)\n    me = motion_energy_trace(obj, me_cells, gocue, ntrials, vidshift)\n    me_vis = np.isfinite(me)\n    timing['video'] = time.time() - t1\n\n    # ---- trial curation ---------------------------------------------------\n    # early-lick and photoinactivation trials are excluded from all analyses in\n    # the reference (params.condition: '~stim.enable & ~early'; Methods:\n    # \"Trials in which the animal contacted the lickport before the reward\n    # ('early lick') were omitted from analyses\").\n    keep = (early < 0.5) & (stim < 0.5) & np.isfinite(gocue)\n    # trials with no spikes at all on any unit = no ephys coverage (the\n    # recording ended before the behavioural session did)\n    spk_per_trial = trialdat.sum(axis=(0, 1))\n    no_ephys = spk_per_trial <= 0\n    keep &= ~no_ephys\n    ntrials_keep = int(keep.sum())\n\n    # ---- outputs ----------------------------------------------------------\n    # lick direction (getPrevChoice.m: choice = (R&hit) | (L&miss))\n    lickdir = np.full(ntrials, 2, dtype=np.int8)                 # none\n    lickdir[((R > 0.5) & (hit > 0.5)) | ((L > 0.5) & (miss > 0.5))] = 1   # right\n    lickdir[((L > 0.5) & (hit > 0.5)) | ((R > 0.5) & (miss > 0.5))] = 0   # left\n    lickdir[no > 0.5] = 2\n\n    context = np.where(autowater > 0.5, 0, 1).astype(np.int8)    # 0=WC, 1=DR\n\n    outcome = np.full(ntrials, -1, dtype=np.int8)\n    outcome[miss > 0.5] = 0                                      # incorrect\n    outcome[hit > 0.5] = 1                                       # correct\n    outcome[no > 0.5] = 2                                        # ignore\n\n    # per-session median thresholds, computed on the retained trials only\n    tng_d, tng_thr = discretize(tongue_speed[keep], tongue_vis[keep])\n    paw_d, paw_thr = discretize(paw_speed[keep], paw_vis[keep])\n    me_d, me_thr = discretize(me[keep], me_vis[keep])\n\n    kt = np.flatnonzero(keep)\n    neural, inputs, outputs = [], [], []\n    inp = TAXIS.astype(np.float32)[None, :]\n    for j, t in enumerate(kt):\n        neural.append(np.ascontiguousarray(trialdat[:, :, t].T))       # (nunits, NT)\n        inputs.append(inp.copy())\n        out = np.empty((6, NT), dtype=np.int8)\n        out[0] = lickdir[t]\n        out[1] = context[t]\n        out[2] = outcome[t]\n        out[3] = tng_d[j]\n        out[4] = paw_d[j]\n        out[5] = me_d[j]\n        outputs.append(out)\n\n    info = dict(\n        animal=anm, date=date, task=task, probes=[int(p) for p in probes],\n        probe_locations=sorted(set(p[1] for p in unit_probe)),\n        file_format=obj.fmt,\n        ntrials_total=int(ntrials), ntrials_kept=ntrials_keep,\n        n_early=int(np.sum(early > 0.5)), n_stim=int(np.sum(stim > 0.5)),\n        n_no_ephys=int(np.sum(no_ephys)),\n        nunits_all=sum(len(qualities[p]) for p in probes),\n        nunits_quality=n_quality, nunits=nunits,\n        # provenance: 0-based indices of the retained trials and of the retained\n        # clusters (index into obj.clu{probe}) in the original data object\n        trial_indices=np.flatnonzero(keep).astype(np.int32),\n        cluster_indices={int(p): np.asarray(keep_clu[p], dtype=np.int32) for p in probes},\n        unit_quality=list(unit_quality),\n        n_single_units=int(sum(q in SINGLE_UNIT_QUALITY for q in unit_quality)),\n        video_offset_s=float(vidshift),\n        median_delay_s=float(np.nanmedian(gocue - delay)),\n        median_sample_s=float(np.nanmedian(delay - sample)),\n        tongue_speed_threshold=float(tng_thr), paw_speed_threshold=float(paw_thr),\n        motion_energy_threshold=float(me_thr),\n        frac_context_WC=float(np.mean(context[keep] == 0)),\n        frac_outcome=[float(np.mean(outcome[keep] == c)) for c in range(3)],\n        frac_lickdir=[float(np.mean(lickdir[keep] == c)) for c in range(3)],\n        frac_tongue_notvisible=float(np.mean(tng_d == 2)),\n        frac_paw_notvisible=float(np.mean(paw_d == 2)),\n        frac_me_novideo=float(np.mean(me_d == 2)),\n        mean_firing_rate=float(np.mean([f for f, u in zip(fr, use) if u])) if nunits else 0.0,\n    )\n\n    if outcome[keep].min() < 0:\n        raise RuntimeError(f'{anm} {date}: trial with no hit/miss/no outcome')\n\n    res = dict(neural=neural, input=inputs, output=outputs,\n               region=unit_region, quality=unit_quality, info=info,\n               subject=anm)\n\n    if show:\n        _plot_processing(outdir, anm, date, obj, gocue, sample, delay, keep, kt,\n                         trialdat, tongue_speed, tongue_vis, tng_thr,\n                         paw_speed, paw_vis, paw_thr, me, me_vis, me_thr,\n                         lickdir, context, outcome, unit_region)\n\n    timing['total'] = time.time() - t0\n    info['timing'] = {k: round(v, 2) for k, v in timing.items()}\n    obj.close()\n    return res\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n#  Diagnostic plots\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\ndef _plot_processing(outdir, anm, date, obj, gocue, sample, delay, keep, kt,\n                     trialdat, tongue_speed, tongue_vis, tng_thr,\n                     paw_speed, paw_vis, paw_thr, me, me_vis, me_thr,\n                     lickdir, context, outcome, unit_region):\n    import matplotlib\n    matplotlib.use('Agg')\n    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n\n    ev_sample = np.median(sample - gocue)\n    ev_delay = np.median(delay - gocue)\n    fig, ax = plt.subplots(4, 2, figsize=(18, 20))\n\n    # (1) raster + smoothed rate for the most active unit, first kept trials\n    rates = trialdat[:, :, keep].mean(axis=(0, 2))\n    u = int(np.argmax(rates))\n    axx = ax[0, 0]\n    ntr = min(40, len(kt))\n    lL, lR = obj.licks('L'), obj.licks('R')\n    for j, t in enumerate(kt[:ntr]):\n        axx.plot(TAXIS, trialdat[:, u, t] / max(1e-9, trialdat[:, u, kt[:ntr]].max()) * 0.9 + j,\n                 lw=0.5, color='k')\n        for lt, c in ((lL[t], 'r'), (lR[t], 'b')):\n            lt = np.asarray(lt).ravel() - gocue[t]\n            lt = lt[(lt > TMIN) & (lt < TMAX)]\n            axx.plot(lt, np.full(lt.size, j + 0.5), '.', color=c, ms=2)\n    for e, c in ((0, 'k'), (ev_sample, 'g'), (ev_delay, 'm')):\n        axx.axvline(e, color=c, ls='--', lw=1)\n    axx.set_title(f'{anm} {date}: unit {u} smoothed rate per trial (black)\\n'\n                  'licks: red=left blue=right; dashed: sample/delay/go cue')\n    axx.set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)'); axx.set_ylabel('trial')\n\n    # (2) population PSTH split by lick direction\n    axx = ax[0, 1]\n    for d, c, lab in ((0, 'r', 'left'), (1, 'b', 'right'), (2, 'gray', 'none')):\n        m = keep & (lickdir == d)\n        if m.sum() > 2:\n            axx.plot(TAXIS, trialdat[:, :, m].mean(axis=(1, 2)), color=c, label=lab)\n    for e, c in ((0, 'k'), (ev_sample, 'g'), (ev_delay, 'm')):\n        axx.axvline(e, color=c, ls='--', lw=1)\n    axx.legend(); axx.set_title('population mean firing rate by lick direction')\n    axx.set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)'); axx.set_ylabel('spk/s')\n\n    # (3) tongue speed + discretisation\n    for row, (sp, vis, thr, name) in enumerate(\n            [(tongue_speed, tongue_vis, tng_thr, 'tongue speed'),\n             (paw_speed, paw_vis, paw_thr, 'paw speed'),\n             (me, me_vis, me_thr, 'motion energy')]):\n        axx = ax[row + 1, 0]\n        for j, t in enumerate(kt[:6]):\n            v = sp[t].copy()\n            axx.plot(TAXIS, v + j * 3 * (thr if np.isfinite(thr) else 1), 'k', lw=0.8)\n            iv = ~vis[t]\n            axx.plot(TAXIS[iv], np.full(iv.sum(), j * 3 * thr), 'r.', ms=2)\n            axx.axhline(j * 3 * thr + thr, color='b', lw=0.5)\n        axx.axvline(0, color='k', ls='--')\n        axx.set_title(f'{name}: traces for 6 trials (blue = per-session median '\n                      f'{thr:.3g}, red = invalid)')\n        axx.set_xlabel('time from go cue (s)')\n\n        axx = ax[row + 1, 1]\n        vals = sp[keep][vis[keep]]\n        axx.hist(vals, bins=100)\n        axx.axvline(thr, color='b')\n        axx.set_yscale('log')\n        axx.set_title(f'{name} distribution over valid samples; median={thr:.3g}; '\n                      f'frac invalid={1 - np.mean(vis[keep]):.3f}')\n\n    fig.tight_layout()\n    fn = os.path.join(outdir, f'processing_{anm}_{date}.png')\n    fig.savefig(fn, dpi=110)\n    plt.close(fig)\n    print(f'  wrote {fn}')\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n#  Main\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\ndef main():\n    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)\n    ap.add_argument('outfile')\n    ap.add_argument('--full', action='store_true', help='process all sessions (default)')\n    ap.add_argument('--sample', action='store_true', help='process only 2 sessions')\n    ap.add_argument('--show-processing', action='store_true',\n                    help='save per-step diagnostic plots for up to 2 sessions')\n    args = ap.parse_args()\n\n    sessions = SESSIONS\n    if args.sample:\n        # one fixed-delay two-context session and one randomized-delay session\n        sessions = [SESSIONS[2], SESSIONS[29]]\n\n    t_start = time.time()\n    data = dict(neural=[], input=[], output=[], subjects=[], subject_idx=[],\n                brain_regions=[], brain_region_idx=[],\n                input_names=INPUT_NAMES, output_names=OUTPUT_NAMES,\n                output_values=OUTPUT_VALUES, metadata={})\n    subjects, regions, session_info = [], [], []\n    nshown = 0\n    for k, (anm, date, probes, datadir, task) in enumerate(sessions):\n        show = args.show_processing and nshown < 2\n        res = process_session(anm, date, probes, datadir, task, show=show,\n                              outdir=os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(args.outfile)) or '.')\n        nshown += int(show)\n        if anm not in subjects:\n            subjects.append(anm)\n        for r in res['region']:\n            if r not in regions:\n                regions.append(r)\n        data['neural'].append(res['neural'])\n        data['input'].append(res['input'])\n        data['output'].append(res['output'])\n        data['subject_idx'].append(subjects.index(anm))\n        data['brain_region_idx'].append(np.array([regions.index(r) for r in res['region']],\n                                                 dtype=np.int64))\n        session_info.append(res['info'])\n        i = res['info']\n        print(f\"[{k + 1}/{len(sessions)}] {anm} {date} ({i['file_format']}) \"\n              f\"units {i['nunits_all']}->{i['nunits_quality']}->{i['nunits']} \"\n              f\"(SU {i['n_single_units']}) | trials {i['ntrials_total']}->{i['ntrials_kept']} \"\n              f\"(early {i['n_early']}, stim {i['n_stim']}, no-ephys {i['n_no_ephys']}) | \"\n              f\"WC {i['frac_context_WC']:.2f} | invalid tongue/paw/me \"\n              f\"{i['frac_tongue_notvisible']:.2f}/{i['frac_paw_notvisible']:.2f}/\"\n              f\"{i['frac_me_novideo']:.2f} | {i['timing']['total']:.1f}s \"\n              f\"{i['timing']}\", flush=True)\n\n    data['subjects'] = subjects\n    data['subject_idx'] = np.array(data['subject_idx'], dtype=np.int64)\n    data['brain_regions'] = regions\n    data['metadata'] = dict(\n        task_description=(\n            'Head-fixed mice performed directional licking tasks. In delayed-response '\n            '(DR) trials an auditory tone (1.3 s sample epoch) instructed a left or right '\n            'lick, which had to be withheld through a delay epoch (0.9 s fixed, or '\n            'randomized 0.3-3.6 s in the randomized-delay sessions) until an auditory go '\n            'cue; correct licks were rewarded with water. In water-cued (WC) trials, '\n            'interleaved in blocks of 10-25 trials in a subset of sessions, all auditory '\n            'cues were omitted and water was delivered at a random port at a random time; '\n            'mice had to infer the context. Neural activity was recorded extracellularly '\n            'with silicon probes in anterior lateral motor cortex (ALM; a few probes were '\n            'located in tongue-jaw motor cortex, tjM1). Decoded variables are the '\n            \"animal's lick direction, the behavioural context, the trial outcome, and \"\n            'discretised tongue speed, paw speed and whole-frame motion energy from '\n            'high-speed video.'),\n        time_bin_size=DT * 1000.0,\n        temporal_alignment_event=('go cue onset (DR trials) / water drop presentation '\n                                  '(WC trials); obj.bp.ev.goCue'),\n        off_start=TMIN,\n        off_end=TMAX,\n        neural_units='spikes/s (spike counts in 10 ms bins, divided by the bin width and '\n                     'smoothed with the causal gaussian kernel of mySmooth.m, N=15 bins, '\n                     'reflect boundary condition)',\n        neuron_curation=('clusters whose manual quality label is garbage/gabrga/noisy/real? '\n                         'are discarded (findClusters.m), then units with mean firing rate '\n                         '<= 1 Hz over the -2.5..2.5 s window across all trials are '\n                         'discarded (removeLowFRClusters.m, params.lowFR = 1)'),\n        trial_curation=('early-lick trials (bp.early) and photoinactivation trials '\n                        '(bp.stim.enable) are excluded, as in the reference conditions '\n                        \"'~stim.enable&~early'; trials with no spikes on any unit (ephys \"\n                        'recording ended before the behavioural session) are excluded'),\n        video='DeepLabCut, 400 Hz, two cameras; aligned to the ephys clock with '\n              'findVideoOffset.m and resampled onto the neural time axis',\n        output_discretisation=('tongue/paw speed and motion energy are split at the 50th '\n                               'percentile of all valid samples of that session; timepoints '\n                               'where DeepLabCut did not track the feature (tongue, paw) or '\n                               'where no video frames cover the timepoint (motion energy) '\n                               'get the third class'),\n        source='Hasnain, Birnbaum et al., Nat Neurosci 2024; Each session is a MATLAB struct `obj` with fields\n    37→`bp` (bpod trial/task data), `clu` (sorted spikes, one cell per probe), `traj` (DeepLabCut\n    38→output, `{1}` = side cam, `{2}` = bottom cam), `sglx` (SpikeGLX metadata), `ex` (session\n    39→metadata incl. probe location), `me` (motion energy; only for behaviour-only sessions).\n    40→\n    41→### Key Functions Identified\n    42→| Function | File | Stage | Purpose |\n    43→|----------|------|-------|---------|\n    44→| `load<ANM>_ALMVideo` | DataLoadingScripts/Recording and video/*.m | LOADING | **Definitive session + probe list**: which sessions have ephys+video and which probe is ALM. Commented-out entries = sessions the authors excluded. |\n    45→| `loadObjs` | DataLoadingScripts/loadObjs.m | LOADING | loads `data_structure_<anm>_<date>.mat` for each entry of `meta` |\n    46→| `loadSessionData` | DataLoadingScripts/loadSessionData.m | LOADING | per session/probe driver; concatenates units of both probes when `meta.probe = [1 2]` |\n    47→| `processData` | DataLoadingScripts/processData.m | PROCESSING | findTrials → findClusters → alignSpikes → getSeq → removeLowFRClusters → baselineFR |\n    48→| `findTrials` | DataLoadingScripts/findTrials.m | CURATION | evaluates condition strings such as `'R&hit&~stim.enable&~autowater&~early'` over `obj.bp` fields |\n    49→| `findClusters` | DataLoadingScripts/findClusters.m | CURATION | with `params.quality={'all'}` keeps every cluster except quality `garbage`, `gabrga`, `noisy`, `real?` |\n    50→| `alignSpikes` | DataLoadingScripts/alignSpikes.m | PROCESSING | `trialtm_aligned = trialtm - ev.(alignEvent)(trial)`; `params.alignEvent='goCue'` |\n    51→| `getSeq` | DataLoadingScripts/getSeq.m | PROCESSING | histograms aligned spikes into `params.tmin:params.dt:params.tmax` bins, divides by `dt` (spikes/s) and smooths with `mySmooth`; produces `obj.trialdat (time, units, trials)` |\n    52→| `mySmooth` | utils/mySmooth.m | PROCESSING | causal gaussian kernel: `gausswin(15)` with first 7 taps zeroed, normalised, `conv(...,'same')`, `'reflect'` boundary = prepend first 15 samples then trim |\n    53→| `removeLowFRClusters` | DataLoadingScripts/removeLowFRClusters.m | CURATION | drops units whose mean firing rate over the window/trials is `<= params.lowFR` |\n    54→| `getDefaultParams` / `WorkingWithDataObjs.m` | DataLoadingScripts | PROCESSING | parameters: `alignEvent='goCue'`, `tmin=-2.5`, `tmax=2.5`, `dt=1/100`, `smooth=15`, `bctype='reflect'`, `lowFR=1`, `quality={'all'}` |\n    55→| `findVideoOffset` | funcs/findVideoOffset.m | PROCESSING | `vidshift = mode(sglx.bitcode.bitstart)/sglx.fs - mode(bp.ev.bitStart)`; video clock → ephys clock |\n    56→| `findPosition` | funcs/kinematics/findPosition.m | PROCESSING | `interp1(frameTimes - vidshift - alignTime, xy, taxis)`; skips trials with `isnan(NdroppedFrames)`;
